Geopolitical markets form clusters where probabilities must satisfy constraints — strike-count markets are cumulative (P(7) &lt;= P(6)), daily military action across regions should correlate, and prerequisite events constrain downstream markets. When these constraints are violated, the mispriced market reverts. This skill detects violations and trades the correction, sizing by conviction.
